HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3cd1d184595b15b9b029","text":"The later specifications of harm prevent 113:2 from remaining a generic totality. 113:3 made created harm environmental and spatial; 113:4 made it technical and mediated; 113:5 made it social and affective. The surprise is that `مَا خَلَقَ` became a compressed inventory of possible harm-channels rather than an abstract \"everything\": formed beings, dark atmospheres, handled knots, emitted breath, and envious persons. 113:5 especially activated `خ ل ق B004`, since the created thing's dangerous property may be character.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7d76193850c64d9ff841","text":"4. **Exploratory: created harm is joined by fabricated harmful constructs.** Dormant fixed **خ ل ق B007** supplies fabricated false speech. It is activated by 113:1 **ق و ل B001** (utterance), 113:4 **ن ف ث B001** (mouth emission), **ع ق د B007** (tangled speech), and **ع ق د B013** (spell-binding). The packet supplies speech, emission, and bound spells; I infer that contrived words can be given a material or ritual carrier. The direct perfect *خَلَقَ* and its proximity to *رب الفلق* favor ordinary creation, so this remains exploratory; nevertheless, the fixed phrase can now cast a secondary shadow toward harms people fabricate rather than merely encounter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_81c318fd2d3aad46a49f","text":"1. **The general harm is unfolded as harmful operation, not as a claim that creation is itself evil.** Fixed **ش ر ر B001** supplies evil or badness and fixed **خ ل ق B002** originating creation. The three following ayat repeat *وَمِن شَرِّ* and then specify darkness when it enters, blowers acting in knots, and an envier when envy is enacted. The packet supplies the broad *ما*, the repeated construction, and those condition/action structures; I infer that the later items unpack ways created agents, conditions, or capacities become harmful. The fixed ayah consequently reads as refuge from harm arising within the created field while leaving creation and harmful actualization distinct.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8521ac590e621f56f2cb","text":"4. **Evil as spark and spread.** Evidence: `ش ر ر B003` flying sparks of fire and `ش ر ر B002` spreading something in the sun; activated by `غ س ق B001` night darkness, `ف ل ق B002` dawn split, and `ق و ل B007` circulating talk. Mechanism: the root inventory lets `شر` carry images of tiny flying fragments and spreading surfaces, while the surah later gives hidden darkness and social circulation. I infer that harm can spread by small emissions rather than by a single large force. Reading change: `شَرِّ` becomes an image of diffusive, spark-like damage as well as moral evil.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004","root_000787/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_89e7a31d4cb28ab315d3","text":"2. **Evil as inner disposition becoming social action.** Evidence: `خ ل ق B004` inner disposition and character; `ش ر ر B001` evil/badness; activated by `ح س د B001` wishing removal of another's blessing, `ع ق د B006/B012` fixed conviction and knotted anger, and `ق و ل B007/B005` circulating report and false attribution. Mechanism: the packet moves from general createdness to concrete hostile agents: envier, knotted intention, and damaging speech. I infer that a created thing's harm can be its moral texture, not only its physical existence. Reading change: `مَا خَلَقَ` includes created characters and social dispositions that become harmful.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ab4e867fdf344b745192","text":"2. **Created form includes an inward disposition whose danger appears in enactment.** Dormant fixed **خ ل ق B004** supplies inner character or disposition, while 113:5 **ح س د B001** supplies a removal-seeking disposition and repeats it as agent *حَاسِد* plus action *حَسَدَ*. The structural cue is the movement from the broad created object in 113:2 to a person/action pair under *إذا* in 113:5. The packet supplies character, actor, and act; I infer that a disposition housed in a created person is not identical to the harm it produces when directed and enacted. *مَا خَلَقَ* thus acquires an interior moral dimension in addition to visible created forms.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ac374acf431a039220aa","text":"3. **Manufactured media of harm.** Evidence: `خ ل ق B001` measuring/proportioning before cutting or doing, `خ ل ق B008` smooth/leveled surfaces, and `ش ر ر B004` shredding/cutting loose; activated by `ع ق د B001/B013` physical and magic knots, `ن ف ث B001` breath-spittle into knots, and `و ق ب B001` apertures/hollows. Mechanism: the packet's later harms are not only persons but worked media: knots, surfaces, openings, breath, and possibly prepared objects. I infer that `created` can include things apportioned, handled, and repurposed as channels of injury. Reading change: the ayah becomes a broad prelude to harm mediated through crafted or manipulated forms.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004","root_000787/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b4d070fb50348cbbe6b1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:2:2\",\"root_000787\",[\"B001\"]],[\"113:2:4\",\"root_000434\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yaratılmış bir kişide bulunan iç eğilim ile bu eğilimin yöneltilip eyleme dönüşmesi birbirinden ayrılır; zarar ikinci aşamada belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b610dfbdc65295eecd52","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:2:4\",\"root_000434\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yaratma sözü, ikincil olarak, insanların söz ve bağlama yoluyla kurduğu yapay zararları da gölgeleyebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B002","root_000434/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c4b218e10fd6ac8cf325","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:2:4\",\"root_000434\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yaratılmış şeylerin açığa çıkıp görünür biçim kazanması, onları aynı zamanda zarar görebilir hâle getiren bir eşik olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B002","root_000787/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ca4988f3a5a725676aaf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:2:2\",\"root_000787\",[\"B001\"]],[\"113:2:4\",\"root_000434\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonraki ayetler, kötülüğü yaratılışın kendisiyle özdeşleştirmek yerine yaratılmış varlık, durum ve imkânların zarara dönüşmesi olarak açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_000434/B003","root_000434/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dea2132d32097ab47f43","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:2:4\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ölçülmüş ve biçimlenmiş şeylerin açıklık, oyuk ve bağlantıları, zararlı eylemin kullanabildiği taşıyıcılara dönüşebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B002","root_000787/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e24877daaa8d413522b3","text":"1. **Createdness as exposed emergence.** Evidence: `ش ر ر B001` evil/badness; `خ ل ق B002` originating and bringing into being and `خ ل ق B003` complete formed shape; activated by `ف ل ق B003` bringing creation out through splitting and `ر ب ب B002` nurture/completion. Mechanism: creation and cleaving share an emergence pattern: something measured or formed comes out and becomes visible. I infer that exposure itself creates vulnerability to harm. Reading change: `مَا خَلَقَ` is not a blanket suspicion of existence; it marks the total exposed field of formed things within which harm can arise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004","root_000434/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_edb6a3737b7499dd8d9f","text":"113:5 supplied the decisive correction to an object-only reading of *مَا خَلَقَ*: its noun-plus-verb repetition made dormant fixed **خ ل ق B004** (inner disposition) visible and separated a created bearer from harmful enactment. 113:4 also unexpectedly activated **خ ل ق B007** (fabricated speech) through mouth emission and spell-binding, although the direct form *خَلَقَ* keeps that second relation exploratory.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000787/B002","root_000787/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ef02e0c1c2245c5184fe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:2:2\",\"root_000787\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kötülük, tek bir büyük kuvvetten çok küçük parçalar hâlinde sıçrayıp yayılan bir zarar biçimi de kazanabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f4b9e436d551436e3b11","text":"3. **Harm exploits the affordances of measured creation.** Fixed **خ ل ق B001** supplies measuring and proportioning, and **خ ل ق B003** supplies completed visible form. Around it, 113:1 **ف ل ق B001** opens a division, 113:3 **و ق ب B001** supplies a receiving recess and **و ق ب B002** entry, and 113:4 **ع ق د B001** fastens ends. These packet relations describe openings, cavities, and joins within formed things; I supply the causal arrow that harmful action can use those designed thresholds to penetrate or bind.
